User notes and answers:
- CONTRACT RESOLUTION (post-cycle-3 escalation): The 4 cross-repo blockers from gate v3 have been resolved as follows. Plan revision should incorporate these as settled facts. (1) WORKER CLAIM PROTOCOL: Do NOT extend supabase/functions/claim-next-task. The orchestrator at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py ALREADY implements the banodoco worker-pool (BANODOCO_POOL constant, banodoco_timeline_generate task type, SD-034 envelope validation, JWKS-verified user_jwt, update_timeline_config_versioned write path, is_worker_pool_available check with BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC default 30s). AA implements the WORKER side of this pool — Python pipeline that polls the orchestrator's task queue, claims banodoco_timeline_generate tasks, verifies user_jwt against Reigh's Supabase JWKS, executes, then writes via update_timeline_config_versioned (the versioned RPC). The reference Node+Python worker image is at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/ — AA can use that as the integration target. (2) TASK-STATUS ENDPOINT: The reigh-app sprint-8 branch (sprint-8-themed-render-via-orchestrator) has been patched locally (commit ee2e6f10c, not yet pushed) to add a NEW supabase/functions/task-status/ edge function: GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>, JWT-authed via _shared/auth.ts, returns {status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}} sourced from tasks.result_data JSON column. This is exactly what pollBanodocoTaskStatus expects. AA does NOT need to call this endpoint itself — reigh-app's poller calls it. AA just writes result_data via update-task-status. (3) UPDATE-TASK-STATUS RESULT FIELD: update-task-status now accepts an optional result_data: Record<string, unknown> in its request body, persisted to tasks.result_data. AA's worker, on completion, calls update-task-status with status='Complete' and result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id} so the new task-status reader can surface those fields. result_data column already existed in tasks (migration 20250202000000_add_missing_columns.sql, jsonb default '{}'). (4) STATUS ENUM CASING: AA adapts to Title Case. Use Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled — these are the only values update-task-status accepts. Do NOT use lowercase. (5) PACKAGE DEPS: @banodoco/timeline-ops and @banodoco/timeline-schema are private and unpublished. For AA's helper Node toolchain (Phase 1 fixture export, Phase 4 ops helpers), use file: dependencies in a root package.json pointing at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema}/typescript. This works for sibling-checkout dev environments (which is the only deployment target right now). NPM publish is a separate deployment decision — out of scope for this megaplan. Helpers that need APIs not exported by the local packages (e.g., createTimelineCommandRunner from reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/commands/index.ts) should be replaced by direct timeline-ops calls (addClip/moveClip/setTimelineTheme/etc) since those primitives ARE exported. PHASE 0 of execute should add this root package.json and verify the file: imports work. (6) DATAPROVIDER METHOD LIST: Tighten Step 5's DataProvider Python interface to mirror the actual TypeScript contract: load_timeline, save_timeline, load_asset_registry, resolve_asset_url, optional register_asset, optional upload_asset, optional load_checkpoints/save_checkpoint, optional load_waveform (no save_waveform), optional load_asset_profile (no load_profile/save_profile). Remove fictional save_waveform/save_profile/load_profile from success criteria.
- CORRECTED FACTS (replan basis): (1) Package file: deps point at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-{ops,schema} — the package.json sits at the package ROOT, not /typescript subdir. (2) Worker write path uses SERVICE-ROLE Supabase client to call the versioned RPC; reference at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/worker_writes.py. user_jwt is verified via JWKS for AUTHORIZATION (in worker_jwt.py) but the actual DB write is service-role. RPC signature: update_timeline_config_versioned(p_timeline_id uuid, p_expected_version int, p_config jsonb) — 3 params, no project_id. (3) Worker claim path: POST /functions/v1/claim-next-task with body {worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}. As of commit f8599a887 on reigh-app sprint-8 branch, this is now supported. Reference at banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130. (4) Worker status report path: POST /functions/v1/update-task-status with {task_id, status:'Complete'|'Failed'|..., output_location?, error?, result_data?:{config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id, ...}}. Status values are Title Case. result_data is a new optional jsonb field as of commit ee2e6f10c. (5) DROP SEQUENCE SUPPORT FROM THIS SPRINT. timeline-schema does NOT export SequenceDraft/validateSequenceDraft/TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES — those live in reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/sequences/ which is not a published package. AA's Phase 5 should be removed. Sequence support comes in a later sprint after that module is published or otherwise made consumable by AA. (6) Reference banodoco-worker module structure at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/: worker.py (claim loop), worker_writes.py (RPC), worker_jwt.py (JWKS verify), worker_assets.py, worker_pipeline.py, worker_remotion.py, worker_health.py. AA's worker can either subclass/extend this reference or reimplement against the same contract. (7) DataProvider TS contract has only loadWaveform?(assetId) and loadAssetProfile?(assetId) — no save_waveform, no load_profile, no save_profile. Tighten the Python interface accordingly. (8) Phase plan: Phase 1: Renderer swap (AA's remotion/src/Root.tsx delegates to <TimelineComposition>); Phase 2: Python DataProvider client (timeline-import write, reigh-data-fetch read, optimistic versioning); Phase 3: AA banodoco worker (claim loop, JWKS verify, dispatch banodoco_timeline_generate to AA executor, write via service-role RPC, status via update-task-status); Phase 4: CLI collapse (artagents project verbs operate on reigh-app project IDs via DataProvider); Phase 5 (REMOVED). Phase 6: Renderer parity smoke tests using sprint-8 fixtures.

  "meta_commentary": "Large multi-phase integration. Six judgment calls the executor needs to attend to: (1) SD-009 auth scope \u2014 the plan's wording broadens service-role usage to non-worker callers (open_in_reigh, project CLI edit verbs); FLAG-012/issue_hints/scope flag this as conflicting with the brief. The executor should prefer user-JWT/PAT for ownership-bound CLI writes and gate service-role to the worker path, documenting the choice in module docstrings. If a unified DataProvider.save_timeline must accept service_role_key for ergonomic reasons, make the auth selector explicit and require the caller to opt into service-role with a named arg. (2) FLAG-013 \u2014 the worker MUST do an explicit project-ownership check (service-role select on projects.user_id) before the RPC; worker_jwt only proves identity, not ownership. T9 and T13 are written to enforce this. (3) FLAG-014 \u2014 when T1 documents the local package internals, the source files live at typescript/src/index.ts, not src/index.ts; the npm file: deps still point at package roots (correct). (4) Phase 2 (T6) is hard-gated on the reigh-app PR (T2) merging \u2014 if the executor wants to land Phase 2 before the PR merges, they need to mock config_version everywhere and gate live integration. (5) Renderer parity goldens are not yet on disk; T14 should skip cleanly rather than fail. (6) All five SD-* invariants (RPC three-arg signature, service-role-only DB write, expected_version required, Title Case statuses, no SequenceDraft work) are tested in T13 \u2014 keep those tests strict; they are the regression backstop. Execution order: T1\u2192T2\u2192(T3,T5 in parallel)\u2192T4\u2192T6\u2192T7,T8\u2192T9\u2192T10\u2192T11\u2192T12\u2192T13,T14\u2192T15. Don't try to short-cut by skipping T1/T2 \u2014 the integration brief and the config_version PR are the artifacts other phases hang off.",

      "description": "Phase 3 Step 8 \u2014 Create artagents/core/reigh/task_client.py mirroring banodoco-worker/worker.py:88-130. `claim_next_task(*, worker_id, service_role_key) -> ClaimResult | None` POSTs to /functions/v1/claim-next-task with body `{worker_id, run_type:'banodoco-worker', worker_pool:'banodoco', task_types:['banodoco_timeline_generate']}` and returns the SD-034 envelope (task_id, run_id, project_id, params, user_jwt, ...) or None. `update_task_status(task_id, *, status, result_data=None, error=None, output_location=None, service_role_key)` POSTs to /functions/v1/update-task-status with status restricted to Title Case enum {Queued, In Progress, Complete, Failed, Cancelled}; result_data is a dict persisted to tasks.result_data. Module docstring records that AA NEVER calls /functions/v1/complete-task and NEVER calls /functions/v1/task-status (those are reigh-app's poller). Create artagents/core/reigh/worker_jwt.py mirroring banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py \u2014 verifies user_jwt against the JWKS at resolve_jwks_url() (signature, audience, expiry); returns VerifiedJwt(user_id, audience, raw_claims). Used FOR AUTHORIZATION ONLY (the DB write itself uses service-role).",

    {
      "id": "T9",
      "description": "Phase 3 Step 9 \u2014 Create artagents/core/worker/__init__.py and artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py. Module docstring records resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with citations to banodoco-worker/worker.py, worker_writes.py, worker_jwt.py, and task-status/index.ts (commit ee2e6f10c). Reception: long-running poll loop calling task_client.claim_next_task. Defensively reject envelopes whose task_type != banodoco_timeline_generate. Authorization: worker_jwt.verify(task.user_jwt); on signature/expiry/audience failure \u2192 update_task_status(status='Failed', error='invalid user_jwt'). FLAG-013/callers/correctness \u2014 ALSO add an explicit project-ownership check mirroring banodoco-worker/worker.py's `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)`: do a service-role read of `projects.user_id` and require it equal verified.user_id BEFORE the RPC write; on mismatch \u2192 status='Failed' with error='project ownership mismatch'. Snapshot fast-path: if params.current_timeline present, dispatch on it; else SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline. Dispatch by `intent` to existing AA executors; each returns a mutator callable. Carry-fields: every emitted clip populates source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order (SD-003) \u2014 no new structured ref field. Local provenance: write runs/<run_id>.json with metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload)) at exactly that path (SD-008). Timeline write: SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id=task.params.timeline_id, mutator=executor_mutator, [REDACTED](), retries=3); expected_version=task.params.expected_version is REQUIRED; force=True is forbidden on this path. Status report on success: update_task_status(task_id, status='Complete', result_data={'config_version': result.new_version, 'correlation_id': params.correlation_id, 'timeline_id': params.timeline_id}). On failure: status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id}. Status enums Title Case ONLY. Wire `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into artagents/pipeline.py:main and _print_entrypoint_help.",

{
  "cmd": "python3 - <<'PY'\nimport json, os, pathlib, re, subprocess, sys\nroot = pathlib.Path([REDACTED])\nplan = root / '.megaplan/plans/wire-artagents-into-reigh-app-20260504-1033/finalize_snapshot.json'\ndata = json.loads(plan.read_text())\nprint('before_execute actions:')\nfor action in data['user_actions']:\n    if action.get('phase') == 'before_execute':\n        print(f\"- {action['id']}: blocks {','.join(action.get('blocks_task_ids', []))}\")\n\ncandidate_envs = []\nfor base in [root, pathlib.Path([REDACTED]), pathlib.Path([REDACTED])]:\n    if base.exists():\n        for p in base.rglob('.env*'):\n            if '.git' not in p.parts and 'node_modules' not in p.parts:\n                candidate_envs.append(p)\nkeys = ['REIGH_SUPABASE_URL', 'SUPABASE_URL', 'REIGH_PAT', 'REIGH_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY', 'REIGH_SUPABASE_JWKS_URL']\nprint('env presence:')\nmissing = []\nfor key in keys:\n    present_sources = []\n    if os.environ.get(key):\n        present_sources.append('process-env')\n    pat = re.compile(rf'^\\s*(?:export\\s+)?{re.escape(key)}\\s*=\\s*(.+)\\s*$')\n    for p in candidate_envs:\n        try:\n            for line in p.read_text(errors='ignore').splitlines():\n                m = pat.match(line)\n                if m and m.group(1).strip().strip('\"\\''):\n                    display = str(p.relative_to(root)) if p.is_relative_to(root) else str(p)\n                    present_sources.append(display)\n                    break\n        except OSError:\n            pass\n    if not present_sources:\n        missing.append(key)\n    print(f\"- {key}: {'SET' if present_sources else 'MISSING'} ({'; '.join(present_sources) if present_sources else 'no process env or .env entry found'})\")\nprint(f\"env files scanned: {len(candidate_envs)}\")\nprint(f\"env verification: {'PASS' if not missing else 'FAIL missing ' + ','.join(missing)}\")\nPY",
  "workdir": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents",

}

AA implements the WORKER side of this pool — Python pipeline that polls the orchestrator's task queue, claims banodoco_timeline_generate tasks, verifies user_jwt against Reigh's Supabase JWKS, executes, then writes via update_timeline_config_versioned (the versioned RPC). The reference Node+Python worker image is at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/ — AA can use that as the integration target. (2) TASK-STATUS ENDPOINT: The reigh-app sprint-8 branch (sprint-8-themed-render-via-orchestrator) has been patched locally (commit ee2e6f10c, not yet pushed) to add a NEW supabase/functions/task-status/ edge function: GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>, JWT-authed via _shared/auth.ts, returns {status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}} sourced from tasks.result_data JSON column. This is exactly what pollBanodocoTaskStatus expects. AA does NOT need to call this endpoint itself — reigh-app's poller calls it. AA just writes result_data via update-task-status.     {
      "id": "T6",
      "description": "Phase 2 Step 6 \u2014 Implement the Python DataProvider stack at artagents/core/reigh/{errors.py, supabase_client.py, timeline_io.py, data_provider.py}. errors.py: TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError. supabase_client.py: thin POST/RPC wrapper with parameterised auth selector `auth=(\"user_jwt\"|\"pat\"|\"service_role\", token)`; expose an `rpc(name, params, auth=...)` helper. timeline_io.py: `save_timeline(*, timeline_id, mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig], service_role_key, retries=3, force=False) -> SaveResult`. Loop: load (timeline, config_version) via reigh-data-fetch; apply mutator; call RPC `update_timeline_config_versioned` with EXACTLY `{p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config}` (three params, NO project_id) and `auth=(\"service_role\", service_role_key)`. On version-mismatch, re-fetch + re-apply, up to retries; raise TimelineVersionConflictError on exhaustion. Reject expected_version=None unless force=True (and log when force is used). data_provider.py: class SupabaseDataProvider with EXACT canonical surface \u2014 required: load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> (TimelineConfig, int), save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3, force=False), load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id), resolve_asset_url(asset_entry); optional: register_asset, upload_asset (writes to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then register_asset), load_checkpoints, save_checkpoint, load_waveform, load_asset_profile. Explicitly NOT present: save_waveform, save_profile, load_profile. Round-trip every fetched timeline through artagents.timeline.validate_timeline / load_timeline / save_timeline so byte-equivalent allowlist parity stays intact. Watch: FLAG-012/issue_hints flagged that broadly handing service_role_key to non-worker callers conflicts with SD-009; treat service_role_key as worker-only and design the DataProvider so the CLI (T11) can call save_timeline with a non-service-role auth path or a different write entrypoint when SD-009 demands it. If the DataProvider must take service_role_key universally, document the deviation explicitly in module docstring.",

      "description": "Phase 3 Step 9 \u2014 Create artagents/core/worker/__init__.py and artagents/core/worker/banodoco_worker.py. Module docstring records resolutions for open contracts #1, #2, #4 with citations to banodoco-worker/worker.py, worker_writes.py, worker_jwt.py, and task-status/index.ts (commit ee2e6f10c). Reception: long-running poll loop calling task_client.claim_next_task. Defensively reject envelopes whose task_type != banodoco_timeline_generate. Authorization: worker_jwt.verify(task.user_jwt); on signature/expiry/audience failure \u2192 update_task_status(status='Failed', error='invalid user_jwt'). FLAG-013/callers/correctness \u2014 ALSO add an explicit project-ownership check mirroring banodoco-worker/worker.py's `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)`: do a service-role read of `projects.user_id` and require it equal verified.user_id BEFORE the RPC write; on mismatch \u2192 status='Failed' with error='project ownership mismatch'. Snapshot fast-path: if params.current_timeline present, dispatch on it; else SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline. Dispatch by `intent` to existing AA executors; each returns a mutator callable. Carry-fields: every emitted clip populates source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order (SD-003) \u2014 no new structured ref field. Local provenance: write runs/<run_id>.json with metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload)) at exactly that path (SD-008). Timeline write: SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id=task.params.timeline_id, mutator=executor_mutator, [REDACTED](), retries=3); expected_version=task.params.expected_version is REQUIRED; force=True is forbidden on this path. Status report on success: update_task_status(task_id, status='Complete', result_data={'config_version': result.new_version, 'correlation_id': params.correlation_id, 'timeline_id': params.timeline_id}). On failure: status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id}. Status enums Title Case ONLY. Wire `python3 -m artagents worker --pool banodoco ...` into artagents/pipeline.py:main and _print_entrypoint_help.",

Do not document or import\n+`packages/timeline-ops/src/index.ts` or `packages/timeline-schema/src/index.ts`;\n+those paths do not exist.\n+\n+## Resolved Contracts\n+\n+### 1. Worker Reception Model\n+\n+ArtAgents implements the worker side of Reigh's existing banodoco worker pool.\n+It should poll `POST /functions/v1/claim-next-task` with:\n+\n+```json\n+{\n+  \"worker_id\": \"<stable worker id>\",\n+  \"run_type\": \"banodoco-worker\",\n+  \"worker_pool\": \"banodoco\",\n+  \"task_types\": [\"banodoco_timeline_generate\"]\n+}\n+```\n+\n+The endpoint supports `worker_pool` and `task_types` filtering, and maps\n+`banodoco-worker` to pool-specific RPC filtering. The reference worker currently\n+claims render tasks too, but AA's current sprint scope is only timeline\n+generation. AA must defensively reject any claimed task whose `task_type` is not\n+`banodoco_timeline_generate`.\n+\n+### 2. JWT Authorization and Service-Role Write Split\n+\n+`user_jwt` is an authorization input, not the DB write credential. AA must:\n+\n+1. Verify `user_jwt` against Reigh Supabase JWKS, checking signature,\n+   `aud=authenticated`, expiry, and `sub`.\n+2. Use the verified JWT subject as the actor identity.\n+3. Perform an explicit service-role read of `projects.user_id` for the task's\n+   `project_id`, and require it to equal the verified JWT subject.\n+4. Use service-role only for the trusted worker write path.\n+5. Call `update_timeline_config_versioned` with exactly:\n+\n+```json\n+{\n+  \"p_timeline_id\": \"<timeline uuid>\",\n+  \"p_expected_version\": 1,\n+  \"p_config\": {}\n+}\n+```\n+\n+Do not pass `project_id` or `p_audited_user_id` to the current Reigh RPC.\n+\n+### 3. Asset Upload and Resolution Contract\n+\n+The storage bucket is `timeline-assets`.\n+\n+Upload path shape is:\n+\n+```text\n+${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}\n+```\n+\n+The Reigh data provider registers the uploaded asset into the timeline's asset\n+registry after upload. Worker render resolution accepts HTTP URLs directly, or\n+downloads storage-key entries from `timeline-assets` using service-role into a\n+local temp path, then supplies Remotion-friendly `file` URLs.\n+\n+### 4. Task Status, `result_data`, and Poller Readback\n+\n+AA must never call `/functions/v1/task-status`; that GET endpoint is for\n+Reigh's poller. AA must write status through `/functions/v1/update-task-status`\n+with Title Case statuses only:\n+\n+```text\n+Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled\n+```\n+\n+On success, AA writes:\n+\n+```json\n+{\n+  \"task_id\": \"<task uuid>\",\n+  \"status\": \"Complete\",\n+  \"result_data\": {\n+    \"config_version\": 2,\n+    \"correlation_id\": \"<correlation uuid>\",\n+    \"timeline_id\": \"<timeline uuid>\"\n+  }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+On failure, AA writes `status: \"Failed\"`, an error field matching the endpoint\n+contract used by implementation, and at minimum `result_data.correlation_id`.\n+The new `task-status` GET reads `tasks.result_data`, hoists\n+`correlation_id/message/failure_code`, and returns other keys under `result`.\n+\n+### 5. Sequence Schema\n+\n+Sequence support is deferred for this sprint. Mechanical search found\n+`SequenceDraft`, `validateSequenceDraft`, and `TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_CLIP_TYPES` in\n+`reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/sequences/*`, not in\n+`@banodoco/timeline-schema`. The local `timeline-schema` package currently\n+exports only `./schemas.js` and `./resolveTheme.js`. AA should not implement\n+SequenceDraft support until that sequence module is published or otherwise made\n+consumable by AA.\n+\n+## Shared Package Version Strategy\n+\n+Use root `file:` dependencies in ArtAgents' root `package.json`:\n+\n+```json\n+{\n+  \"dependencies\": {\n+    \"@banodoco/timeline-ops\": \"file:/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops\",\n+    \"@banodoco/timeline-schema\": \"file:/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-schema\"\n+  }\n+}\n+```\n+\n+Do not point dependencies at `/typescript` subdirectories. The package root\n+`package.json` files own `main`, `types`, and `exports`. Source citations and\n+debugging should use `typescript/src/index.ts`.\n+\n+## Phase Gates and Follow-On Notes\n+\n+- T2 must patch `reigh-data-fetch` so `TIMELINES_SELECT` includes\n+  `config_version`; without it, AA cannot load `expected_version` from the\n+  read side and Phase 2 cannot validate live optimistic writes.\n+- Renderer parity goldens are not present yet; renderer parity tests should\n+  skip with an explicit reason when goldens are absent.\n+- Non-worker CLI writes must preserve the SD-009 auth boundary. Prefer PAT or\n+  user-JWT paths for user-owned authoring flows, reserving service-role for the\n+  trusted AA worker.\n*** End Patch\n"
}

(1) WORKER CLAIM PROTOCOL: Do NOT extend supabase/functions/claim-next-task. The orchestrator at /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker-orchestrator/api_orchestrator/handlers/banodoco.py ALREADY implements the banodoco worker-pool (BANODOCO_POOL constant, banodoco_timeline_generate task type, SD-034 envelope validation, JWKS-verified user_jwt, update_timeline_config_versioned write path, is_worker_pool_available check with BANODOCO_WORKER_UNAVAILABLE_TIMEOUT_SEC default 30s). AA implements the WORKER side of this pool — Python pipeline that polls the orchestrator's task queue, claims banodoco_timeline_generate tasks, verifies user_jwt against Reigh's Supabase JWKS, executes, then writes via update_timeline_config_versioned (the versioned RPC). The reference Node+Python worker image is at banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/ — AA can use that as the integration target. (2) TASK-STATUS ENDPOINT: The reigh-app sprint-8 branch (sprint-8-themed-render-via-orchestrator) has been patched locally (commit ee2e6f10c, not yet pushed) to add a NEW supabase/functions/task-status/ edge function: GET /functions/v1/task-status?task_id=<uuid>, JWT-authed via _shared/auth.ts, returns {status, correlation_id?, message?, failure_code?, result?: {config_version?, timeline_id?, ...}} sourced from tasks.result_data JSON column. This is exactly what pollBanodocoTaskStatus expects. AA does NOT need to call this endpoint itself — reigh-app's poller calls it. AA just writes result_data via update-task-status. (3) UPDATE-TASK-STATUS RESULT FIELD: update-task-status now accepts an optional result_data: Record<string, unknown> in its request body, persisted to tasks.result_data. AA's worker, on completion, calls update-task-status with status='Complete' and result_data={config_version, correlation_id, timeline_id} so the new task-status reader can surface those fields. result_data column already existed in tasks (migration 20250202000000_add_missing_columns.sql, jsonb default '{}'). (4) STATUS ENUM CASING: AA adapts to Title Case. Use Queued | In Progress | Complete | Failed | Cancelled — these are the only values update-task-status accepts. Do NOT use lowercase. (5) PACKAGE DEPS: @banodoco/timeline-ops and @banodoco/timeline-schema are private and unpublished.       "id": "T6",
      "description": "Phase 2 Step 6 \u2014 Implement the Python DataProvider stack at artagents/core/reigh/{errors.py, supabase_client.py, timeline_io.py, data_provider.py}. errors.py: TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError. supabase_client.py: thin POST/RPC wrapper with parameterised auth selector `auth=(\"user_jwt\"|\"pat\"|\"service_role\", token)`; expose an `rpc(name, params, auth=...)` helper. timeline_io.py: `save_timeline(*, timeline_id, mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig], service_role_key, retries=3, force=False) -> SaveResult`. Loop: load (timeline, config_version) via reigh-data-fetch; apply mutator; call RPC `update_timeline_config_versioned` with EXACTLY `{p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config}` (three params, NO project_id) and `auth=(\"service_role\", service_role_key)`. On version-mismatch, re-fetch + re-apply, up to retries; raise TimelineVersionConflictError on exhaustion. Reject expected_version=None unless force=True (and log when force is used). data_provider.py: class SupabaseDataProvider with EXACT canonical surface \u2014 required: load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> (TimelineConfig, int), save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3, force=False), load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id), resolve_asset_url(asset_entry); optional: register_asset, upload_asset (writes to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then register_asset), load_checkpoints, save_checkpoint, load_waveform, load_asset_profile. Explicitly NOT present: save_waveform, save_profile, load_profile. Round-trip every fetched timeline through artagents.timeline.validate_timeline / load_timeline / save_timeline so byte-equivalent allowlist parity stays intact. Watch: FLAG-012/issue_hints flagged that broadly handing service_role_key to non-worker callers conflicts with SD-009; treat service_role_key as worker-only and design the DataProvider so the CLI (T11) can call save_timeline with a non-service-role auth path or a different write entrypoint when SD-009 demands it.   "meta_commentary": "Large multi-phase integration. Six judgment calls the executor needs to attend to: (1) SD-009 auth scope \u2014 the plan's wording broadens service-role usage to non-worker callers (open_in_reigh, project CLI edit verbs); FLAG-012/issue_hints/scope flag this as conflicting with the brief. The executor should prefer user-JWT/PAT for ownership-bound CLI writes and gate service-role to the worker path, documenting the choice in module docstrings. If a unified DataProvider.save_timeline must accept service_role_key for ergonomic reasons, make the auth selector explicit and require the caller to opt into service-role with a named arg. (2) FLAG-013 \u2014 the worker MUST do an explicit project-ownership check (service-role select on projects.user_id) before the RPC; worker_jwt only proves identity, not ownership. T9 and T13 are written to enforce this. (3) FLAG-014 \u2014 when T1 documents the local package internals, the source files live at typescript/src/index.ts, not src/index.ts; the npm file: deps still point at package roots (correct). (4) Phase 2 (T6) is hard-gated on the reigh-app PR (T2) merging \u2014 if the executor wants to land Phase 2 before the PR merges, they need to mock config_version everywhere and gate live integration. (5) Renderer parity goldens are not yet on disk; T14 should skip cleanly rather than fail. (6) All five SD-* invariants (RPC three-arg signature, service-role-only DB write, expected_version required, Title Case statuses, no SequenceDraft work) are tested in T13 \u2014 keep those tests strict; they are the regression backstop. Execution order: T1\u2192T2\u2192(T3,T5 in parallel)\u2192T4\u2192T6\u2192T7,T8\u2192T9\u2192T10\u2192T11\u2192T12\u2192T13,T14\u2192T15. Don't try to short-cut by skipping T1/T2 \u2014 the integration brief and the config_version PR are the artifacts other phases hang off.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked v5's worker auth story against `/users/user_c042661f/documents/banodoco-workspace/banodoco-worker/worker_jwt.py` and `worker.py`. `worker_jwt.verify_user_jwt` validates signature/audience/expiry and returns the jwt subject; it does not prove project or timeline ownership. the reference worker separately calls `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)` via a service-role read of `projects.user_id` before writing. v5 says `worker_jwt.py` confirms the user owns the timeline and only tests missing ownership claims, but it does not add the separate project-ownership check, which is required when the terminal write uses service-role. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] auth-scope: auth scope: v5 broadens service-role timeline writes from the worker-only path into generic supabasedataprovider, open_in_reigh, and project cli edit flows, conflicting with sd-009's user-jwt/pat ownership-bound cli model. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements: did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked v5 against sd-009 and the corrected user notes. the corrected facts only establish the service-role rpc path for the trusted aa worker, but v5 makes the generic `supabasedataprovider.save_timeline`, `open_in_reigh` default flow, and project cli edit verbs all take `service_role_key` and call the versioned rpc. that broadens service-role use beyond the worker and conflicts with the brief's user-jwt/pat ownership-bound cli path. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: checked the step 1 source-reference paths for the local packages. the package manifests live at the package roots, but the source files are under `typescript/src/index.ts`; `packages/timeline-ops/src/index.ts` and `packages/timeline-schema/src/index.ts` do not exist. v5 says to read `../banodoco-workspace/packages/timeline-ops/{package.json,src/index.ts}` and the matching schema path, so that documentation step still points at missing source files even though the install targets are fixed. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them: find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the reference worker call chain for write authorization. in `banodoco-worker/worker.py`, after jwt verification the caller creates `supabasetimelinerpc(audited_user_id=verified.user_id)` and then calls `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)` before the service-role write. v5's worker caller path lacks that explicit ownership-verification call and relies on `worker_jwt.py` to prove ownership, which the reference `worker_jwt.py` does not do. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

    {
      "id": "T6",
      "description": "Phase 2 Step 6 \u2014 Implement the Python DataProvider stack at artagents/core/reigh/{errors.py, supabase_client.py, timeline_io.py, data_provider.py}. errors.py: TimelineVersionConflictError, TimelineNotFoundError. supabase_client.py: thin POST/RPC wrapper with parameterised auth selector `auth=(\"user_jwt\"|\"pat\"|\"service_role\", token)`; expose an `rpc(name, params, auth=...)` helper. timeline_io.py: `save_timeline(*, timeline_id, mutator: Callable[[TimelineConfig, int], TimelineConfig], service_role_key, retries=3, force=False) -> SaveResult`. Loop: load (timeline, config_version) via reigh-data-fetch; apply mutator; call RPC `update_timeline_config_versioned` with EXACTLY `{p_timeline_id, p_expected_version, p_config}` (three params, NO project_id) and `auth=(\"service_role\", service_role_key)`. On version-mismatch, re-fetch + re-apply, up to retries; raise TimelineVersionConflictError on exhaustion. Reject expected_version=None unless force=True (and log when force is used). data_provider.py: class SupabaseDataProvider with EXACT canonical surface \u2014 required: load_timeline(project_id, timeline_id) -> (TimelineConfig, int), save_timeline(timeline_id, mutator, service_role_key, retries=3, force=False), load_asset_registry(project_id, timeline_id), resolve_asset_url(asset_entry); optional: register_asset, upload_asset (writes to `timeline-assets/${user_id}/${timeline_id}/${epoch_ms}-${filename}` then register_asset), load_checkpoints, save_checkpoint, load_waveform, load_asset_profile. Explicitly NOT present: save_waveform, save_profile, load_profile. Round-trip every fetched timeline through artagents.timeline.validate_timeline / load_timeline / save_timeline so byte-equivalent allowlist parity stays intact. Reception: long-running poll loop calling task_client.claim_next_task. Defensively reject envelopes whose task_type != banodoco_timeline_generate. Authorization: worker_jwt.verify(task.user_jwt); on signature/expiry/audience failure \u2192 update_task_status(status='Failed', error='invalid user_jwt'). FLAG-013/callers/correctness \u2014 ALSO add an explicit project-ownership check mirroring banodoco-worker/worker.py's `_verify_project_ownership(rpc, project_id, verified.user_id)`: do a service-role read of `projects.user_id` and require it equal verified.user_id BEFORE the RPC write; on mismatch \u2192 status='Failed' with error='project ownership mismatch'. Snapshot fast-path: if params.current_timeline present, dispatch on it; else SupabaseDataProvider.load_timeline. Dispatch by `intent` to existing AA executors; each returns a mutator callable. Carry-fields: every emitted clip populates source_uuid, generation, pool_id, clip_order (SD-003) \u2014 no new structured ref field. Local provenance: write runs/<run_id>.json with metadata.baseline_snapshot = sha256_hex(canonical_json(clip_or_payload)) at exactly that path (SD-008). Timeline write: SupabaseDataProvider.save_timeline(timeline_id=task.params.timeline_id, mutator=executor_mutator, [REDACTED](), retries=3); expected_version=task.params.expected_version is REQUIRED; force=True is forbidden on this path. Status report on success: update_task_status(task_id, status='Complete', result_data={'config_version': result.new_version, 'correlation_id': params.correlation_id, 'timeline_id': params.timeline_id}). On failure: status='Failed', error=str(exc), result_data={'correlation_id': params.correlation_id}. Status enums Title Case ONLY. Six judgment calls the executor needs to attend to: (1) SD-009 auth scope \u2014 the plan's wording broadens service-role usage to non-worker callers (open_in_reigh, project CLI edit verbs); FLAG-012/issue_hints/scope flag this as conflicting with the brief. If a unified DataProvider.save_timeline must accept service_role_key for ergonomic reasons, make the auth selector explicit and require the caller to opt into service-role with a named arg. (2) FLAG-013 \u2014 the worker MUST do an explicit project-ownership check (service-role select on projects.user_id) before the RPC; worker_jwt only proves identity, not ownership. T9 and T13 are written to enforce this. (3) FLAG-014 \u2014 when T1 documents the local package internals, the source files live at typescript/src/index.ts, not src/index.ts; the npm file: deps still point at package roots (correct).
